We propose Reduced Matrix Multiplication (RMM), a training-free, input-adaptive inference method that reduces Transformer matrix products by selecting informative slices along their contraction dimensions, without modifying model weights. Under a simple retention-ratio control, RMM provides a smooth and predictable accuracy-efficiency trade-off. Across language models ranging from 1B to 70B parameters, we find that reduction tolerance depends on the model family, task, component, and retention ratio, although it often improves with model scale. Under moderate reduction, RMM remains robust across the evaluated discriminative, autoregressive generation, and long-context settings. We further show that the same principle extends to multimodal vision-language inference. Mechanistic ablations reveal a structural asymmetry within Transformers: attention-side computations are substantially more reducible than MLP components. Finally, wall-clock benchmarks with custom kernels on an NVIDIA A100 show that these computational savings can translate into practical runtime gains, especially at longer sequence lengths. Together, these results position RMM as a scalable direction for input-adaptive inference-time optimization.

</details>


### [237] [Symmetry-Breaking De Novo Crystal Generation via Markovian Jump Diffusion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13457)
*Van Khoa Nguyen,Alexandros Kalousis*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于对称性自发破缺原理的扩散生成框架SbCD（Symmetry-breaking Crystal Diffusion），通过马尔可夫跳跃-扩散过程从最低对称性先验反向生成完整的晶体结构规格，实现了空间群之间的显式物理驱动转换，在MP20和MPTS-52上显著优于保持对称性的基线模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有晶体生成模型只能生成到位点对称性级别，且依赖从经验分布中采样空间群，无法捕捉全局对称性和结构依赖关系，限制了生成晶体的完整性与物理合理性。

Method: 受物理学中自发对称性破缺启发，采用马尔可夫跳跃-扩散过程建模对称性破缺动力学，从最低对称性先验出发逆向生成完整结构规格，显式引入空间群间转移，使模型能够以物理驱动的方式在不同空间群之间遍历。

Result: 在MP20和MPTS-52数据集上的从头生成实验中，SbCD相比保持对称性的对照模型取得了大幅度的性能提升。

Conclusion: SbCD提供了一种有前景的晶体材料生成建模视角，通过物理启发的对称性破缺机制有效提升完整晶体结构生成的质量。

Abstract: Generating crystals has recently attracted significant interest due to their broad applications in materials science. However, existing generative models struggle to produce complete crystallographic specifications, limiting their ability to capture global symmetry and structural dependencies. In particular, current state-of-the-art approaches generate crystals only up to site symmetries and rely on sampling space groups from empirical distributions during generation. Inspired by \emph{spontaneous symmetry breaking} in physics, where crystals break symmetries under external conditions, we propose a novel diffusion-based framework that generates full structure specifications by reversing from the lowest-symmetry priors. Our method leverages a Markovian jump-diffusion process to model these symmetry-breaking dynamics, enabling it to traverse different space groups in a physically motivated manner. Our model, dubbed \emph{Symmetry-breaking Crystal Diffusion} (SbCD), introduces a principled approach to explicitly incorporate inter-space-group transitions into the generative process. In de novo generation experiments on MP20 and MPTS-52, SbCD outperforms its symmetry-preserving counterpart by a substantial margin, offering a promising perspective for generative modeling of crystalline materials.

</details>


### [238] [Doubly Robust Estimation of Causal Effect on CVR with Targeted Regularization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13461)
*Jiayi Dan,Bo Li,Lu Deng,Yong Wang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文从半参数理论角度重新审视CVR因果效应估计问题，提出了一种针对链式结果的doubly robust因果效应估计器，并基于targeted regularization设计了实用框架，在合成和真实数据上验证了有效性和鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: CVR是电子商务和广告中的关键指标，但直接应用现有因果推断方法到点击样本会引入样本选择偏差和方差增大。现有基于"理想损失"的CVR预测方法虽然优化了全样本的无偏损失估计，但损失的无偏性并不能保证最终估计量的无偏性，因此需要从更严谨的理论角度重新设计估计器。

Method: 从半参数理论出发，针对CVR这类链式结构结果开发了新的doubly robust因果效应估计器，详细推导了其理论性质（包括比nuisance参数估计更快的收敛速度，对灵活非参数估计器如神经网络更鲁棒）。基于理论发现，进一步设计了基于targeted regularization的框架以提升数值稳定性和实际可用性。

Result: 在合成和真实数据上的大量实验证明了方法的有效性和鲁棒性。同时发现，简单地将损失去偏与标准因果估计器结合的效果不如所提方法，突显了为CVR目标量身定制且具有坚实理论保证的新估计器的必要性。

Conclusion: 面向CVR的链式结构因果效应估计需要专门的doubly robust估计器，而非简单组合现有方法；所提方法在理论性质和实证表现上均优于现有方案。

Abstract: Post-click conversion rate (CVR) is a key metric in various scenarios including e-commerce and advertising, reflecting the efficiency and user experience in the second stage of the conversion process. Estimating the causal effect on CVR is therefore of great practical importance. However, directly applying existing causal inference methods to clicked samples introduces sample selection bias and increased variance due to the exclusion of non-click data. Recent studies on CVR prediction introduce "ideal loss", which optimizes model parameters using an unbiased estimate of the loss over the full sample. Nevertheless, there is no guarantee that unbiasedness of the loss implies unbiasedness of the final estimator.
  We revisit this challenge from the perspective of semiparametric theory. Specifically, we develop a new doubly robust causal effect estimator for chain-structured outcomes such as CVR, and derive its theoretical properties in detail. It achieves a faster convergence rate compared to nuisance parameters estimation and is therefore more robust when using flexible nonparametric estimators, including neural networks. Based on these theoretical findings, we further design a framework based on targeted regularization to improve numerical stability and practical applicability.
  Extensive experiments on synthetic and real-world data demonstrate the effectiveness and robustness of our method. In addition, we find that naively combining loss debiasing with standard causal estimators underperforms our method, highlighting the necessity of developing the new estimator tailored to this CVR-style objective with solid theoretical guarantees.

</details>


### [239] [Concept Drift Detection and Adaptive Retraining of Malware Classification Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13465)
*Christofer Washington Berruz Chungata,Martin Jurecek,Katerina Potika,William B. Andreopoulos,Mark Stamp*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文分析并比较了两种基于机器学习的自动概念漂移检测方法（OCSVM和MK-Means）以及统计方法MMD，在恶意软件分类模型中的应用效果，通过四种学习模型和三种重训练场景的广泛实验，发现漂移感知重训练方案能在保持与周期性重训练相当分类精度的同时大幅提升训练效率，其中OCSVM方法综合表现最优。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 恶意软件检测模型因攻击者不断修改恶意软件而面临概念漂移问题，导致模型性能随时间下降。频繁重训练成本高昂，而静态模型又无法应对漂移，因此需要有效的概念漂移检测机制来指导模型的及时重训练。

Method: 将概念漂移检测任务分为三种技术路线：基于OCSVM的新型方法、基于MK-Means的已有方法和统计检验方法MMD。采用四种学习模型（MLP、随机森林、SVM、XGBoost），在三种场景（静态无重训练、周期性强制重训练、漂移感知按需重训练）下进行系统对比实验，并通过Pareto Front分析评估漂移感知场景中分类精度与训练效率之间的权衡。

Result: 三种概念漂移检测技术的分类精度与周期性重训练相当，但所需重训练的模型数量显著减少，训练效率大幅提升。其中基于OCSVM的漂移感知重训练方法在整体性能上优于MK-Means和MMD方法。

Conclusion: 研究结果表明，概念漂移可以被准确检测并用于驱动恶意软件分类模型的按需重训练，在保持高分类精度的同时显著降低训练开销，OCSVM方法提供了最佳的精度-效率平衡。

Abstract: Concept drift refers to changes over time in the statistical properties of data, as compared to the data that was used to train a learning model. Machine learning models for malware detection or classification are particularly susceptible to performance degradation caused by concept drift, as attackers constantly modify existing malware. In this chapter, we analyze two machine learning-based approaches to automated concept drift detection-a novel approach based on One-Class Support Vector Machines (OCSVM) and a previously-studied technique based on Minibatch K-Means (MK-Means). For comparison we also consider Maximum Mean Discrepancy (MMD), a statistical technique for detecting changes in multidimensional data. We conduct an extensive series of experiments comparing the effectiveness of four learning models, namely, Multilayer Perceptron, Random Forest, Support Vector Machines, and eXtreme Gradient Boosting. For each of these models, we consider three distinct scenarios: A static scenario where no model retraining occurs, a periodic scenario where models are constantly retrained irrespective of concept drift, and a drift-aware scenario where models are only retrained when concept drift is detected. Under the drift-aware scenario, we analyze the tradeoff between accuracy and training efficiency using Pareto Front analysis. We find that all three concept drift detection techniques achieve classification accuracy comparable to periodic retraining, while offering substantially greater efficiency in terms of the number of models that must be retrained. In addition, drift-aware retraining based on our OCSVM technique generally outperforms the MK-Means and MMD approaches. Overall, these results provide strong evidence that we can accurately detect concept drift in malware classification models.

</details>


### [240] [Synthetic Persona Pretraining: Alignment from Token Zero](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13482)
*Julian Minder,Viktor Moskvoretskii,Raghav Singhal,Difan Jiao,Andy Arditi,Shaobo Cui,Yiderigun Borjigin,Kartik Bali,Stefan Krsteski,Harsh Raj,Huu Nguyen,Jannik Brinkmann,Ashton Anderson,Roland Aydin,Robert West*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种名为"合成人格预训练"（SPP）的新范式，在预训练的token 0阶段即安装期望的助手人格，通过在预训练文档上标注符合价值观的第一人称反思、使用标准交叉熵损失联合训练、并在后训练阶段通过"人格绑定"将期望人格与助手身份绑定。在最多3B参数、500B token的预训练规模下，SPP提升了宪法遵循与越狱鲁棒性，降低了OOD道德困境中的错位率，同时保持能力不降。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前对齐通常在预训练之后才引入，导致价值观成为"薄薄的一层覆盖物"而非深深扎根于模型之中，且容易在后训练阶段发生再次错位。作者希望探索一种新范式：从token 0起就在预训练中植入期望的助手人格，使价值观在预训练阶段就深度内化。

Method: SPP包含三个步骤：1）基于规范性价值宪法，为预训练文档标注符合价值对齐的第一人称反思；2）使用标准交叉熵损失在预训练文档及其反思上联合预训练，在众多人格中安装期望人格；3）在后训练阶段用用户-助手对话数据训练，将期望人格绑定到助手身份上（persona binding）。

Result: 在最高3B参数、500B token的预训练下，SPP提高了宪法遵循能力和越狱鲁棒性，降低了OOD道德困境中的错位率，同时保持基础能力。对比实验显示，仅在预训练末尾引入SPP（而非从头注入）会导致宪法遵循较弱、无法改变价值优先级、困境选择不够对齐；该优势依赖人格绑定，并随预训练预算增加而增强。

Conclusion: 早期塑造价值观对于对齐至关重要，预训练阶段的人格干预是一种有效的对齐手段，验证了"从token零开始对齐"这一范式的可行性与优越性。

Abstract: As language-model-based AI is increasingly deployed in autonomous settings, aligning its goals and values with those of humans becomes critical. Today, alignment, and the assistant identity itself, are typically introduced only after pretraining, once behavioral priors are already established. This can make values a thin overlay, rather than deeply rooted, and facilitate subsequent misalignment. Pursuing a different paradigm, we introduce Synthetic Persona Pretraining (SPP), which installs the desired assistant persona from token zero in pretraining. First, we annotate pretraining documents with value-aligned first-person reflections derived from a normative value constitution. Second, we pretrain via the standard cross-entropy loss on standard pretraining documents as well as their reflections, which installs the desired persona among a multitude of other personas. Finally, we post-train on user-assistant dialogue data, which binds this desired persona to the assistant identity, a process we call persona binding. By pretraining models up to 3B parameters on 500B tokens, we show that SPP improves constitution following and jailbreak robustness, and reduces the misalignment rate in out-of-distribution moral dilemmas, while preserving capabilities. Early intervention matters: compared with alignment from token zero, introducing SPP only at the end of pretraining yields weaker constitution adherence, does not shift value priorities, and leads to less aligned choices in dilemmas. This advantage depends on persona binding and, importantly, increases with pretraining budget. Overall, our results show that shaping values early is critical for alignment and establish pretraining-time persona interventions as an effective approach to do so.

</details>


### [241] [Active-Trace Complexity Bounds for Moreau--Yosida Unadjusted Langevin Sampling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13467)
*Yuchen Xin,Zhihua Zhang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究非光滑复合目标分布下Moreau-Yosida未调整Langevin算法（MYULA）的离散化误差控制。关键发现是：MYULA的主导离散化误差由参考活动迹B_ref控制（即热子步沿π_λ的平均Hessian迹），而非全局曲率界d/λ。对于结构化惩罚（分段线性、lasso型、分组、全变差），曲率-管估计使B_ref与λ无关，从而迭代复杂度从O(ε^{-3})改进到O(ε^{-2})。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典的MYULA收敛分析依赖于Moreau包络的光滑性参数1/λ作为全局Lipschitz常数，这导致误差界中包含全局曲率界d/λ，在λ较小时（为减少Moreau偏差需选取小λ）会导致O(ε^{-3})的次优复杂度。作者旨在利用问题结构（非光滑项g的稀疏/结构特性）导出更精确的曲率信息，从而改进收敛率。

Method: 作者引入了参考活动迹B_ref，定义为从π_λ出发的一个MYULA热子步中a_λ=tr H_λ（Moreau包络g_λ的弱Hessian迹）的平均值。通过证明MYULA主导离散化误差由B_ref控制而非全局上界d/λ，并进一步对结构化惩罚建立曲率-管估计（curvature-tube estimates）来证明B_ref与λ无关。同时导出Moreau偏差界√m W_2(π_λ,π) ≤ G^2λ/4。

Result: 当选择λ≍ε/G^2时，通用估计B_ref≤d/λ给出O(ε^{-3})的精度依赖（与经典结果一致）；但对分段线性、lasso型、分组和全变差等结构化惩罚，曲率-管估计使得B_ref独立于λ，从而在同一MYULA核下获得O(ε^{-2})的改进复杂度。同时给出了端到端的完整保证。

Conclusion: MYULA对非光滑复合目标的收敛分析可以被显著改进：通过B_ref这一参考活动迹取代全局曲率界，能够更精细地刻画实际曲率信息。对广泛使用的结构化惩罚，无需修改算法核即可将精度依赖从ε^{-3}提升到ε^{-2}，这缩小了理论与实践经验之间的差距。

Abstract: We study the Moreau--Yosida unadjusted Langevin algorithm (MYULA) for the nonsmooth composite target \[ π(dx)\propto \exp\{-f(x)-g(x)\}\,dx, \qquad x\in\mathbb R^d, \] where \(f\) is \(m\)-strongly convex with \(L_f\)-Lipschitz gradient and \(g\) is convex and \(G\)-Lipschitz. Let \(g_λ\) be the Moreau envelope of \(g\), \(π_λ\) the corresponding smoothed target, and \(a_λ=\operatorname{tr}H_λ\), where \(H_λ\) is the a.e./weak Hessian of \(g_λ\). We show that the leading MYULA discretization error is controlled by the reference active trace \(B_{\mathrm{ref}}\), the average of \(a_λ\) along the heat substep of one MYULA update started from \(π_λ\), rather than by the global curvature bound \(d/λ\). If \(M_λ\) is an a.e. upper bound for \(a_λ\), then, up to logarithmic factors, \[ N \lesssim \frac{1}{m} \left[ L_f + \frac{ τ_f+G^2+B_{\mathrm{ref}} }{ \varepsilon_{\mathrm{alg}}^2 } + \frac{M_λ}{\varepsilon_{\mathrm{alg}}} \right], \qquad τ_f:= \sup_x\operatorname{tr}\nabla^2 f(x), \] iterations suffice to ensure \(\sqrt m\,W_2(μ_N,π_λ)\leq\varepsilon_{\mathrm{alg}}\), where \(μ_N\) is the law of the \(N\)-th iterate and \(W_2\) is the quadratic Wasserstein distance. We also prove the Moreau-bias bound \[ \sqrt m\,W_2(π_λ,π) \leq \frac{G^2λ}{4}. \] Thus, choosing \(λ\asymp\varepsilon/G^2\) gives an end-to-end guarantee for \(π\). The universal estimate \(B_{\mathrm{ref}}\leq d/λ\) yields \(\widetilde O(\varepsilon^{-3})\) accuracy dependence. For the structured piecewise-linear, lasso-type, group, and total-variation penalties considered here, curvature--tube estimates make \(B_{\mathrm{ref}}\) independent of \(λ\), yielding \(\widetilde O(\varepsilon^{-2})\) for the same classical MYULA kernel.

</details>


### [242] [Intern-S2-Preview: Scientific Agentic Foundation Model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13505)
*Lei Bai,Jiaqi Cao,Chiyu Chen,Guanzhou Chen,Kai Chen,Guangran Cheng,Erfei Cui,Xuanlang Dai,Shengyuan Ding,Shangheng Du,Yanhui Duan,Yue Fan,Youqing Fang,Quan Gan,Yuanyuan Gao,Jiaye Ge,Lixin Gu,Yuzhe Gu,Qipeng Guo,Junjun He,Xin Hong,Ming Hu,Zhouqi Hua,Haian Huang,Junhao Huang,Zixian Huang,Minxi Jin,Lingkai Kong,Alexander Lam,Zehao Li,Zonglin Li,Tianhao Liang,Dahua Lin,Junyao Lin,Tianyang Lin,Zhouhan Lin,Jiangning Liu,Jin Liu,Kuikun Liu,Wenran Liu,Yifei Liu,Yuhong Liu,Yuhong Liu,Zhoumianze Liu,Ziyan Liu,Ziyu Liu,Haijun Lv,Han Lv,Chengqi Lyu,Le Ma,Ningsheng Ma,Zerun Ma,Haoyang Peng,Runyu Peng,Jifei Shan,Zixin Shang,Kou Shi,Xiang Shi,Qisheng Su,Xuerui Su,Hao Sun,Xiao Sun,Yanan Sun,Yu Sun,Huanze Tang,Yinghao Tang,Wenhui Tian,Zhongbo Tian,Bingli Wang,Haomin Wang,Jiarui Wang,Jingzhi Wang,Rui Wang,Xiquan Wang,Yi Wang,Zhecan Wang,Ziyi Wang,Zun Wang,Rubin Wei,Lianyi Wu,Wen Wu,Yue Wu,Yuhan Wu,Zhenyu Wu,Zijian Wu,Shuhao Xing,Jun Xu,Xingle Xu,Xuenan Xu,Xiangchao Yan,Ziang Yan,Bowen Yang,Danni Yang,Lin Yang,Zhiqi Yang,Qian Yao,Haochen Ye,Peng Ye,Jinhui Yin,Jiashuo Yu,Dingbo Yuan,Fei Yuan,Yuhang Zang,Bo Zhang,Chao Zhang,Chen Zhang,Hongjie Zhang,Junming Zhang,Wenlong Zhang,Wenwei Zhang,Yiming Zhang,Zhuo Zhang,Ziyang Zhang,Haiteng Zhao,Penghao Zhao,Yibo Zhao,Zhonghan Zhao,Zhihang Zhong,Bowen Zhou,Peiheng Zhou,Xin Zhou,Xinyu Zhou,Yunhua Zhou,Dongsheng Zhu,Yicheng Zou*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文介绍了Intern-S2-Preview系列科学智能体基础模型，通过科学多模态预训练和统一的后训练流水线（包括监督微调、多任务RL、黑/白盒智能体RL和蒸馏），构建了支持多模态科学理解、推理、生成和长时程任务的397B参数模型，并探索了时间序列建模和Memory Decoder两条记忆增强路径。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 科学发现日益需要AI系统具备跨异构模态科学证据的推理能力、与科学工具和环境的交互能力，以及跨长任务时程的持续进展能力。现有模型在这些方面仍存在不足，亟需专门面向科学场景的智能体基础模型。

Method: 训练流水线包括：(1) 科学多模态预训练，覆盖渲染科学文档、交错图文数据和多样化科学语料；(2) 统一后训练流水线，包含监督微调、可扩展多任务强化学习、黑盒和白盒智能体RL及on-policy蒸馏；配套技术包括partial rollout与off-policy correction、自适应长度正则化、在线投机解码、鲁棒多任务优化和trace-aware experience assembly。架构上，Intern-S2-Preview-397B扩展时间序列建模至数值预测，Memory Decoder作为独立记忆增强路径实现快速科学特化。

Result: 在科学、多模态、智能体和通用基准上的评估显示，Intern-S2-Preview-397B在多个设置下取得有竞争力或领先的结果。时间序列模块提升了SciTS上的科学信号理解和预测；独立的Intern-MemDec-4B扩展将Biology-Instructions平均分从56.92提升至60.32（不修改冻结的397B骨干）。

Conclusion: Intern-S2-Preview系列展示了面向科学发现的智能体基础模型的有效训练范式，其中统一的后训练流水线和多种工程优化手段对模型性能提升至关重要。时间序列建模和Memory Decoder分别提供了科学信号处理与快速领域特化的可行路径。

Abstract: Scientific discovery increasingly requires AI systems that can reason over scientific evidence of heterogeneous modalities, interact with scientific tools and environments, and sustain progress across long task horizons. We present Intern-S2-Preview, a series of scientific agentic foundation models designed to support multimodal scientific understanding, reasoning, generation, and long-horizon tasks. The training pipeline begins with scientific multimodal pre-training over rendered scientific documents, interleaved image-text data, and diverse scientific corpora. Starting from the pretrained checkpoint, we apply a unified post-training pipeline consisting of supervised fine-tuning, scalable multi-task reinforcement learning (RL), black- and white-box agentic RL, and on-policy distillation. This pipeline is supported by practical techniques that improve rollout and training stability and efficiency, including partial rollout with off-policy correction, adaptive length regularization, online speculative decoding, robust multi-task optimization, and trace-aware experience assembly for agentic tasks. At the architecture level, Intern-S2-Preview-397B extends time series modelling from efficient long-sequence understanding to numerical forecasting, while Memory Decoder is studied as a separate memory-augmented path for rapid scientific specialization without modifying the frozen 397B backbone. Evaluations across scientific, multimodal, agentic, and general-purpose benchmarks show that Intern-S2-Preview-397B achieves competitive or leading results in multiple settings. The time series modules improve scientific signal understanding and forecasting on SciTS, while the separate Intern-MemDec-4B extension improves the Biology-Instructions average score from 56.92 to 60.32 without modifying the frozen 397B backbone.

</details>


### [243] [Sparse Orthogonal Regression Technique: A Spectral Framework for Equation Discovery, Approximation, and Integration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13504)
*Sabin Roman,Ljupco Todorovski,Saso Dzeroski*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了稀疏正交回归技术（SORT），一种用于从噪声和不规则采样数据中学习正交基展开的稀疏谱框架。SORT通过L1正则化回归直接从观测中估计展开系数，避免了显式求积或解析内积计算，核心应用于数据驱动的常微分方程发现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的符号回归、语法驱动发现和SINDy类稀疏识别方法在处理有限库遗漏关键非线性项时往往脆弱失效；此外，显式求积和解析内积在噪声和不规则采样数据下难以计算。作者希望通过先恢复紧凑的谱表示，为后续搜索更简单的解析形式提供可靠中间表示。

Method: SORT将向量场表示在选定的正交基中，通过L1正则化回归直接从观测数据估计展开系数，避免显式数值积分或解析内积。该方法将基础设计作为显式的科学建模环节，取代了在通用有限库中进行脆弱选择的方式，并保持低阶主导系数随模型阶数增大的持续性。

Result: 在动力学系统实验中，当基与问题良好适配时，SORT匹配或优于基于库的稀疏回归基线；在稀疏采样、噪声导数估计和表示失配情况下退化更稳定。低阶主导系数随模型阶数增加而保持，支持阶数一致的模型增长。此外，学习到的展开还支持非线性逼近和高维积分估计。

Conclusion: SORT提供了用于系统辨识、逼近和积分的可复用中间表示，并将基础设计作为科学建模问题的显式组成部分，为符号回归和SINDy提供了一种互补的替代路线。

Abstract: We develop the Sparse Orthogonal Regression Technique (SORT), a sparse spectral framework for learning orthonormal-basis expansions from noisy and irregularly sampled data. SORT estimates expansion coefficients directly from observations using L1-regularized regression, avoiding explicit quadrature or analytic inner-product evaluation. The central application is data-driven discovery of ordinary differential equations: vector fields are represented in chosen orthogonal bases and learned as sparse coefficient expansions. This provides a complementary route to symbolic regression, grammar-based discovery, and SINDy-style sparse identification by first recovering a compact spectral representation, which can later guide searches for simpler analytic forms. Across the dynamical-system experiments, SORT matches or improves upon library-based sparse-regression baselines when the basis is well adapted to the problem, and shows more stable degradation under sparse sampling, noisy derivative estimates, and representation mismatch. Specific examples illustrate why this representation is useful: if a finite library misses the problem-specific nonlinearity, the resulting model can fail. SORT is not immune to mismatch, but it shifts the problem away from brittle selection among generic terms to basis design adapted to the problem domain. The experiments also show that dominant low-order coefficients persist as model order increases, supporting order-consistent model growth. Beyond equation discovery, the same learned expansion supports nonlinear approximation and estimation of complex, high-dimensional integrals by coefficient readout. Overall, SORT provides a reusable intermediate representation for system identification, approximation, and integration, while making basis design an explicit part of the scientific modeling problem.

</details>


### [244] [Intervention-Aware Clinical World Model for Post-Op Outcome Forecasting in Cardiology](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13518)
*Yunsung Chung,Yingshuo Liu,Abboud F. Hassan,Han Feng,Mary M. Maleckar,Nassir Marrouche,Jihun Hamm*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一种干预感知的临床世界模型，将患者表示为结构化潜在状态，并通过时间有序的术后事件逐步演化该状态，应用于房颤消融术后的复发风险预测。模型在DECAAF-II内部交叉验证中达到AUROC 0.756和AUPRC 0.777，且无需随访MRI即可实现疤痕范围预测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统临床预测模型将术后结局视为从基线测量到未来终点的单步映射，忽视了恢复过程中非规律轨迹中包含的丰富临床信息（如非同步记录的观察、药物调整、重复干预和生理测量），这些信息随时间推移会动态改变风险评估。

Method: 构建干预感知的临床世界模型：首先将基线影像编码为3D空间潜在状态，然后利用手术上下文、静态协变量、经过时间和围事件生理嵌入来逐步更新该状态；随访影像仅作为训练时的潜在预测目标的监督信号（训练时监督、推理时不需要）。

Result: 在DECAAF-II数据集上，90天恢复窗口内的非规律术后记录为长期复发风险提供了临床有意义的证据。重复内部交叉验证中，复发预测达到AUROC 0.756和AUPRC 0.777；疤痕范围预测MAE为2.971个百分点，推理时无需随访MRI强度。

Conclusion: 所学潜在状态支持不同时间跨度的复发风险查询以及消隐期记录的回顾性输入编辑，表明该模型不仅具备预测能力，还能提供可解释的干预后动态风险评估工具。

Abstract: Many clinical prediction models treat post-intervention outcomes as a one-step mapping from baseline measurements to a future endpoint. However, recovery after a procedure often unfolds as an irregular trajectory: clinical observations, medication changes, repeat interventions, and physiological measurements are recorded asynchronously and can change risk assessment over time. We propose an intervention-aware clinical world model that represents each patient with a structured latent state and evolves it through time-ordered post-intervention events. The model first encodes baseline imaging into a 3D spatial latent state. It then updates this state using procedural context, static covariates, elapsed time, and peri-event physiological embeddings. Follow-up imaging provides training-only supervision through a latent forecasting objective. We apply the framework to atrial fibrillation ablation. During the 90-day recovery window, irregular post-procedure records provide clinically meaningful evidence for long-term recurrence risk. In repeated internal cross-validation on DECAAF-II, our model achieves AUROC 0.756 and AUPRC 0.777 for recurrence prediction. It also achieves a scar-extent MAE of 2.971 percentage points without requiring follow-up MRI intensities at inference. The learned state supports recurrence-risk queries at different horizons and retrospective input editing of blanking-period records.

</details>


### [245] [The data geometry of masking diffusion: Certified-optimal schedules via unmasking growth complexity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13520)
*Martin J. Wainwright*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究掩码扩散在离散采样中的应用，提出了一种路径解析的数据几何度量——"解掩码增长复杂度"(UGC)。UGC的局部增量直接控制KL离散化误差，统一分析伯努利子集和固定基数解掩码方案，并据此设计出可认证最优的采样器与优化的调度策略。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 离散扩散模型中的掩码过程是生成离散数据的重要工具，但现有分析缺乏对数据几何结构的精细刻画，导致调度策略（如单块/多块解掩码计划）难以做到最优，也无法量化由于适应数据几何而带来的增益。作者希望通过引入一种路径相关的复杂度度量，统一并优化离散扩散的采样理论。

Method: 1) 提出UGC（解掩码增长复杂度），作为沿解掩码轨迹的路径相关几何度量；2) 在log-reveal-odds坐标下分析KL离散化误差，推导出优化后的单块和多块调度方案；3) 通过耦合揭示轨迹上的KL增量来估计UGC增量，构建满足高概率KL误差约束的认证最优采样器；4) 将UGC路径塌缩得到聚合UGC质量，连接经典多元依赖度量与离散扩散先前的复杂度分析；5) 在细划分极限下分析平方根UGC密度的平方积分决定最优Euler离散化误差。

Result: 理论与实验结果表明，该方法可以获得接近可预测最优过程的迭代复杂度（仅差常数因子），并实现了与数据几何相适应的自适应调度。示例显示相比粗粒度调度有显著的维度相关增益，包括使用常数个自适应放置块即可实现约Ω(√d)的改进。

Conclusion: UGC提供了一种统一且精细的框架，用于理解掩码扩散的离散化误差、优化调度策略并设计可认证最优的采样算法，同时与经典多元依赖度量和该领域先前的复杂度分析建立了理论联系。

Abstract: We study masking diffusion for discrete sampling and introduce a path-resolved measure of data geometry called the \emph{unmasking growth complexity} ({\textsf{UGC}\xspace}). Its local increments directly control Kullback--Leibler (KL) discretization error, yielding a unified analysis of Bernoulli-subset and fixed-cardinality unmasking schemes. In log-reveal-odds coordinates, this structure yields optimized single-block and multi-block schedules, and quantifies the gains from adapting computational effort to data geometry. Crucially, we show how {\textsf{UGC}\xspace} increments can be estimated from samples via KL increments along coupled reveal trajectories. This leads to \emph{certified-optimal} samplers that achieve a prescribed KL error with high probability and iteration complexity within a constant factor of the corresponding oracle procedure. Collapsing the \ugc path yields the aggregate {\textsf{UGC}\xspace} mass, which connects to classical multivariate dependence measures and complexity measures from previous analyses of discrete diffusion. In the fine-partition limit, the squared integral of the square-root {\textsf{UGC}\xspace} density determines the sharp leading-order optimal Euler discretization error. Examples exhibit substantial dimension-dependent gains over coarse schedules, including $\widetildeΩ(\sqrt{d})$ improvements achievable with a constant number of adaptively placed blocks.

</details>


### [246] [Vero: Can AI Agents Build Formally Verified Software Repositories?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13522)
*Zhe Ye,Hantao Lou,Yuechun Sun,Peiyang Song,Zhengxu Yan,Timothe Kasriel,Qingyang Zhang,Kaiyu Yang,Soonho Kong,Jingxuan He,Dawn Song*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: Vero是首个在仓库级别评估联合实现与证明合成能力的基准，包含43个来自真实项目的多模块Lean 4实例。最强智能体仅完全解决27/43个实例，表明当前智能体在规模化验证软件合成方面仍存在明显不足。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有AI编程智能体无法保证生成代码的正确性，验证代码生成虽能通过机器可检查证明提供更强保障，但已有基准要么聚焦单个函数，要么只评估给定实现下的证明生成，未能检验智能体在真实多模块代码库中做出连贯的实现与证明决策的能力。

Method: Vero构建了43个源自真实软件仓库的多模块实例，覆盖Python、Dafny、Verus和Coq生态，统一迁移到Lean 4并预定义API接口、手工整理的正式规范和参考实现，支持仅证明与代码加证明两种评估模式；同时设计审计机制，允许智能体证明规范不可满足或参考代码不正确，以暴露和修正潜在错误。

Result: 评估多种前沿编码智能体配置（配备Lean工具链），最强的智能体仅完全解决43个实例中的27个，且在最难的仓库上未闭合任何规范，表明现有智能体在仓库级验证软件合成任务上仍存在显著差距。

Conclusion: Vero为衡量朝向仓库级验证软件合成的进展提供了具体测试平台，当前智能体在该任务上仍力有不逮；基准、整理流程和评估框架已开源。

Abstract: AI agents are increasingly used for programming, but do not provide any guarantee on the correctness of generated code. Verified code generation, in which an agent produces both an implementation and a machine-checked proof of its specification, offers a stronger path toward trustworthy AI-generated software. Existing benchmarks in this direction either focus on individual functions or only evaluate proof generation with provided implementations. It is still an open question whether agents can make coherent implementation and proof choices across real multi-module codebases. To bridge this gap, we introduce Vero, the first benchmark to evaluate joint implementation and proof synthesis at the repository level. Vero contains 43 multi-module instances sourced from real-world repositories spanning Python, Dafny, Verus, and Coq, and covering diverse domains from cryptographic protocols to distributed systems. Each instance consists of a multi-module Lean 4 repository with predetermined API interfaces, manually curated formal specifications, and reference implementations, supporting both proof-only and code-and-proof evaluation modes. To improve benchmark reliability, Vero also includes an audit mechanism where agents are allowed to formally prove unsatisfiability of provided specification or incorrectness of reference code, which surfaces and corrects latent code and specification errors during curation. We evaluate frontier coding-agent configurations with Lean toolchain access. The strongest agent fully solves only 27 of 43 instances and closes no specifications on the hardest repositories. Vero provides a concrete testbed for measuring progress toward repository-scale verified software synthesis, where current agents still fall short. We release the benchmark, curation pipeline, and evaluation harness at https://github.com/sunblaze-ucb/vero.

</details>


### [247] [DARTree: Speculative Diffusion Decoding with Autoregressive Draft Trees](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13524)
*Tianyi Li,Yaxin Luo,Xinyi Shang,Zhiqiang Shen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: DARTree提出了一种无需训练的投机解码方法，将预训练的AR校正头从单链扩展至树结构，通过固定宽度候选树和最优优先剪枝实现批量并行推理，在7个基准上取得最高的平均接受长度和加速比。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 投机解码通过并行验证多个草稿token实现无损加速，但扩散式草稿模型的逐位置分布是边缘分布而非沿草稿路径的条件分布。现有循环校正仅在单条草稿链上引入因果信息，而扩散式树构建虽扩大了候选覆盖范围却未沿各分支传递校正信息。因此需要一种方法将AR校正头从链扩展到树，在保持因果校正的同时利用树的候选覆盖优势。

Method: DARTree首先通过单批次展开并评分每个深度上的所有节点来构建固定宽度候选树，然后仅施加最优优先剪枝来选择验证树，从而将AR校正头推理与顺序堆操作解耦，避免串行化的性能瓶颈。

Result: 在数学、代码和对话共7个基准上，DARTree在全部四种模型-温度配置中取得了最高的平均接受长度和加速比。每轮验证最多接受12.97个token，比DFlash高98.6%，比Domino高27.9%，相比本地测量的自回归解码最高达到9.73倍无损加速。

Conclusion: DARTree成功将AR校正从单链扩展到树结构，通过批量构建候选树与最优优先剪枝的分离设计，兼顾了树结构的候选覆盖广度和因果校正的准确性，在多个基准上实现了最先进的投机解码性能。

Abstract: Speculative decoding losslessly accelerates autoregressive language models by verifying multiple draft tokens in parallel. Diffusion-based drafters further reduce proposal latency by predicting an entire token block in parallel, but their position-wise distributions are marginal rather than conditioned on tokens selected along each draft path. Existing recurrent correction incorporates causal information along a single draft chain, whereas diffusion-based tree construction broadens candidate coverage without carrying this correction along individual branches. We introduce DARTree, a training-free speculative decoding method that extends a pretrained AR correction head from chains to trees. DARTree first constructs a fixed-width candidate tree by expanding and scoring all nodes at each depth in a single batch, and then only applies best-first pruning to select the verification tree, decoupling AR-head inference from sequential heap operations. Across seven math, code, and chat benchmarks, DARTree achieves the highest average acceptance length and speedup in all four model--temperature configurations, accepting up to 12.97 tokens per verification round, 98.6\% more than DFlash and 27.9\% more than Domino in the same setting, and reaching up to 9.73$\times$ lossless speedup over locally measured autoregressive decoding.

</details>


### [248] [Exponential Convex Calibration Dimension for the Multi-Label Jaccard Measure](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13549)
*Mingyuan Zhang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究了多标签分类中逐实例Jaccard分数（IoU）的校准性质。证明Jaccard损失矩阵非奇异且仿射维数为2^s-1，精确校准的锥维数（CCdim）介于2^{s-1}到2^s-1之间，因此任何精确校准的凸替代损失都需要指数数量的预测坐标。同时给出两个多项式维度的近似保证：F1-to-Jaccard转移实现渐近Jaccard遗憾不超过3-2√2；MinHash平方损失替代可在任意条件标签分布上实现任意固定的加性遗憾容忍度，维度为多项式级别。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Jaccard分数（IoU）是多标签分类和二元分割中的标准评估指标，但对其损失矩阵的结构性质（如非奇异性、仿射维数）以及凸替代损失实现校准所需的最小预测维度缺乏严格的理论刻画。核心问题在于：精确校准是否必然需要指数维度，还是存在多项式维度的可行方案。

Method: 本文采用三重技术手段：(1) 利用有限MinHash Gram表示与布尔Möbius反演相结合，证明Jaccard、shifted-loss和普通损失矩阵的非奇异性及仿射维数为2^s-1；(2) 对于精确校准下界，构造因子加权分布，支持2^{s-1}+1个结果和贝叶斯最优报告，从而建立CCdim下界；(3) 对于近似保证，提出F1-to-Jaccard遗憾转移定理以及基于MinHash平方损失的两种构造（直接形式和带符号变体），并给出显式遗憾转移界。

Result: 证明了Jaccard/shifted-loss/ordinary损失矩阵均非奇异，损失列仿射维数为2^s-1；精确校准下2^{s-1} ≤ CCdim(L^Jac) ≤ 2^s-1。任何精确校准的凸替代都需要指数预测坐标。F1-to-Jaccard转移将现有(s^2+1)维F1替代转化为渐近Jaccard遗憾≤3-2√2的多项式时间规则。MinHash平方损失替代可对任意条件标签分布一致地实现Jaccard遗憾下限α：直接构造维度O((s^2+s·log(1/ρ))/α^2)，带符号变体维度O((s+log(1/ρ))/α^2)。

Conclusion: 零遗憾校准需要指数维度，而任意固定的加性遗憾容忍度都可通过多项式预测维度实现。这一结果在精确校准与近似校准之间划出了一条清晰的复杂度分界线，为多标签分类中Jaccard指标的实际应用提供了理论基础。

Abstract: The per-instance Jaccard score, or intersection over union (IoU), is standard in multi-label classification and binary segmentation. With $s$ labels, its loss matrix has $2^s$ outcomes and reports. Under the convention $\mathrm{Jac}(\varnothing,\varnothing)=1$, we prove that the Jaccard score, shifted-loss, and ordinary loss matrices are nonsingular and that the loss columns have affine dimension $2^s-1$. The proof combines a finite MinHash Gram representation with Boolean Möbius inversion. For exact calibration, we prove $2^{s-1} \leq \mathrm{CCdim}(L^{\mathrm{Jac}}) \leq 2^s-1$. The lower bound uses a factorially weighted distribution with $2^{s-1}+1$ supported outcomes and Bayes-optimal reports. Consequently, every exactly calibrated convex surrogate requires exponentially many prediction coordinates. We also give two polynomial-dimensional approximation guarantees with explicit regret transfers. A new $F_1$-to-Jaccard transfer turns an existing $(s^2+1)$-dimensional $F_1$ surrogate into a polynomial-time rule with asymptotic Jaccard regret at most $3-2\sqrt{2}$. For any $α>0$ and $0<ρ<1$, a MinHash square-loss surrogate attains Jaccard-regret floor $α$ uniformly over arbitrary conditional label distributions. With probability at least $1-ρ$, the direct construction has dimension $O((s^2+s\log(1/ρ))/α^2)$, while a signed variant has dimension $O((s+\log(1/ρ))/α^2)$. Thus zero-regret calibration requires exponential dimension, whereas every fixed additive regret tolerance admits polynomial prediction dimension.

</details>


### [249] [Defensive Boosting for Online Probabilistic Forecasting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13554)
*Georgy Noarov,Aaron Roth*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出"Defensive Booster"算法，这是一个针对自适应对手的在线二分类概率预测框架，同时获得两种不兼容的保证：在线梯度提升的Brier分数竞争性以及在线弱到强提升的分类误差率保证。算法利用boosting的"对偶视角"，在分类误差持续偏高时生成硬核证书证明弱学习条件失败。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有在线提升方法只能分别提供两种互不兼容的保证：在线梯度提升在Brier分数上与H的span竞争，但当span中无准确预测器时无任何保证；在线弱到强提升在弱学习条件下将分类误差驱至零，但当该条件不成立时几乎无保证。需要一个统一框架同时获得两种保证。

Method: 提出Defensive Booster算法，通过操作化boosting的"对偶视图"：当算法的随机化分类误差持续偏高时，其错误权重形成平滑重加权，使得每个弱假设在该重加权上具有低边（low edge），从而产生事后的硬核证书证明弱学习条件失败。同时算法仅访问一个弱学习器，而非维护大集合的弱学习器集成。还开发了强自适应变体，在每个时间区间上同时满足两种保证。

Result: Defensive Booster在每条自适应序列上以与在线梯度提升相同的速率在Brier分数上与H的span中最佳预测竞争；同时当真实转录满足平滑弱学习条件时，其Brier分数与随机化分类误差满足与在线分类提升相同的速率保证。实验表明其在合成和真实数据流上表现优异（有时显著优于所有先前基线），且运行时间快数个数量级。

Conclusion: Defensive Booster提供了一个统一且高效的在线提升框架，首次同时实现梯度提升和弱到强提升的双重保证，并且只需访问单个弱学习器，在理论上和实验上都优于先前需维护大型集成的方法。

Abstract: We study online probabilistic forecasting of binary outcomes chosen by an adaptive adversary. Given an online learning algorithm for a weak hypothesis class $H$, we would like to efficiently obtain two incomparable guarantees that existing online boosting techniques provide separately. Online gradient boosting competes in Brier score with the best predictor induced by the span of $H$ on every sequence, but promises nothing when the span does not contain an accurate predictor. Online weak-to-strong boosting drives classification error to zero under a weak-learning condition, but promises little when that condition fails.
  We give a simple defensive forecasting algorithm, the Defensive Booster, that obtains both guarantees. On every adaptive sequence, its Brier score is competitive with the best prediction induced by the span of $H$ at the same rate as online gradient boosting; simultaneously, whenever the realized transcript satisfies the smooth weak-learning condition, its Brier score and randomized classification error satisfy the same rate guarantee as online classification boosting. This is achieved by operationalizing the "dual view" of boosting: When the algorithm's randomized classification error is persistently high, its mistake weights form a smooth reweighting on which every weak hypothesis has low edge, yielding an ex-post hard-core certificate that the weak-learning condition fails. We also develop a strongly adaptive variant, which satisfies both guarantees on every time interval. The Defensive Booster is very efficient: it accesses just one weak-class learner, whereas the prior online boosting methods we compare against maintain large weak-learner ensembles. Experiments on synthetic and real data streams demonstrate its strong predictive performance (sometimes substantially improving over all prior baselines) coupled with orders-of-magnitude faster runtime.

</details>


<div id='math.OC'></div>

# math.OC [[Back]](#toc)

### [250] [Weight Certificates for Convex Multi-Objective MPC: Geometric Characterization, $\ell^1$ Construction, and $\ell^2$ Foreclosure](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12520)
*Hadi Hajieghrary,Benedikt Walter,Chaitanya Shinde,Miguel Hurtadoand Jerry Lopez*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文研究了自动驾驶规则手册中字典序规则排序与加权和模型预测控制之间的精确性关系。作者证明：加权和能精确复现字典序最优解的充要条件是权重（加上单位性能系数）位于成就映射上像在外法向锥上的单位性能切片中，该切片在铰链惩罚下是一个多面体，可通过线性规划和缩放KKT系统求得。平方铰链惩罚下无有限权重可精确复现（误差以O(1/w)衰减）。实验表明所得权重在某些场景下表现优于启发式权重，但证书是逐点有效的，在单个episode内无法保证全程有效。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自动驾驶规则手册将违规行为按字典序排序，模型预测控制（MPC）要么通过每周期L+1个顺序程序精确执行，要么通过分离启发式w1≫w2≫...≫wL的加权和近似执行。然而，这种大权差距启发式是否真正解决了正确的数学问题并不清楚。作者试图从数学上严格刻画加权和精确复现字典序最优解的条件，并回答启发式是否恰当。

Method: 本文采用凸优化理论分析。对凸优先级排序程序，作者证明加权和复现字典序最优的充要条件是其权重（叠加单位性能系数）支撑字典序点的成就映射的上像，允许权重构成外法向锥的单位性能切片。在铰链（hinge）惩罚下，该切片是通过缩放KKT系统投影得到的多面体，线性规划可返回带认证边距的内部权重。平方铰链惩罚下，当极限乘子非零时无有限权重可精确，局部极小分支上的违规以O(1/w)衰减。实验在25条规则的nuPlan闭环设置和保留日志上校准。

Result: 校准后得到的权重在11个可校准场景类中的9个中层级组件近似相等；闭环nuPlan实验中，合法层级事件精度约为匹配启发式权重的两倍。但证书是逐点性质的：没有任何单一权重在同一个episode的所有采样tick上有效，中位寿命仅为一个采样间隔（原生采样率下后续tick为零），持续性追踪活跃集稳定性。

Conclusion: 分离启发式w1≫w2≫...≫wL回答了错误的问题。精确加权和需要满足特定的几何条件（上像支撑条件），且该条件仅在逐点意义上成立。作者建议采用有监控的加权求解器配合选择性级联回退机制，但合规模式监控器只能检测到部分实测的失稳。这揭示了当前字典序加权MPC在实际部署中的根本性局限。

Abstract: Automated-driving rulebooks rank rule violations lexicographically, and model predictive control enforces that ranking either exactly, through $L{+}1$ sequential programs per tick, or approximately, through a weighted sum tuned by the separation heuristic $w_1\gg w_2\gg\cdots\gg w_L$. We show the heuristic answers the wrong question. For a convex priority-ordered program, a weighted sum reproduces the lexicographic optimum precisely when its weight, augmented by a unit performance coefficient, supports the upper image of the achievement map at the lexicographic point; the admissible weights form the unit-performance slice of an outward normal cone. Under hinge penalties this slice is a polyhedron obtained by projecting a scaled-KKT system, and a linear program returns an interior weight with a certified margin; under squared-hinge penalties no finite weight is exact whenever the limiting multiplier is nonzero, with violation along the local minimizer branch decaying as $O(1/w)$. Calibrated on held-out logs, the resulting weights have near-equal tier components in nine of eleven calibration-eligible scenario classes and roughly double legal-tier event precision against a matched heuristic weight in closed-loop nuPlan experiments on a 25-rule rulebook. The certificate is, however, pointwise: no single weight is valid across the sampled ticks of an episode, the median lifetime is one sampling interval (zero subsequent ticks at the native rate), and persistence tracks active-set stability. These findings motivate monitored weighted solves with selective cascade fallback, although the compliance-pattern monitor detects only a subset of measured lapses.

</details>


### [251] [Optimal Heat Storage Sizing for District Heating Networks to Maximize Electricity Revenue from Combined Heat and Power Units](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12525)
*Martin Sollich,Maarten Blommaert*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于数学优化的可扩展自动化方法，用于优化区域供热网络（DHN）中的短期储热规模。该方法基于非线性物理模型，同时建模DHN、热生产者和储热装置，以最小化总经济成本。在比利时一个拥有30个用户的第三代热电联产区域供热网络上验证，最优储热集成使20年总成本从441万欧元降至368万欧元，降低16.5%（73万欧元）。与简化的储热定容方法对比，证明了所提整体物理优化方法在确保可行设计、准确成本评估和最优储热定容方面的优势。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 区域供热网络中集成储热有助于管理热需求波动、能源价格变化和可再生能源间歇性，特别是在热电联产（CHP）系统中可通过储热将热提取转移到电价有利时段。尽管储热的好处已被充分认识，但由于热源、生产消费模式、热网损失和燃料成本的多样性，确定最优储热规模仍然具有挑战性。现有研究缺乏可扩展的自动化方法论来解决这一问题。

Method: 提出一种基于数学优化的可扩展自动化方法，采用非线性物理模型同时建模区域供热网络、热生产者和储热装置，通过最优储热规模选择最小化总经济成本。该方法显式考虑时变热生产成本、热需求和热损失，并在30个用户的第三代CHP区域供热网络上进行验证，超越了以往物理基础储热定容研究的规模。

Result: 在30个用户的比利时CHP-DHN系统中，最优储热集成使20年成本从441万欧元降至368万欧元，降低16.5%（73万欧元）。其中热生产成本降低107万欧元（通过将热提取转移到低电价时段），储热投资成本为32.3万欧元。与常用的简化储热定容方法对比，简化方法无法识别最优储热规模，而所提的整体物理优化方法能够确保可行设计、准确成本评估和最优储热定容。

Conclusion: 所提出的基于物理模型的数学优化方法能够自动确定区域供热网络中最优的短期储热规模，在超越以往研究规模的真实案例中实现了显著的成本节约（16.5%），并证明了与传统简化方法相比，整体物理优化方法在设计可行性、成本评估准确性和储热定容最优性方面具有明确优势。

Abstract: Integrating heat storages in district heating networks (DHNs) supports managing dynamic characteristics such as heat-demand variations and changing energy prices, and the intermittency of renewable sources. A common application are DHNs with combined heat and power (CHP) units, where a storage allows shifting heat extraction to periods with favorable electricity prices. Although the benefits of heat storage in DHNs are well established, determining the optimal storage size remains challenging due to the diversity of DHNs in terms of heat sources, production and consumption patterns, network heat losses, and fuel costs. This paper presents a scalable, automated methodology for optimizing short-term heat storage in DHNs using mathematical optimization. The nonlinear, physics-based approach models the DHN, heat producers, and storages simultaneously to minimize total economic cost through optimal storage sizing, explicitly considering time-varying heat-production costs, heat demands, and heat losses. The methodology is demonstrated on a 3rd-generation CHP-DHN in Belgium with 30 consumers, exceeding the scale of previous physics-based storage-sizing studies. For this system, optimal storage integration reduces the 20-year cost by 730 k EUR (16.5%), from 4.41 M EUR to 3.68 M EUR. The reduction results from a 1.07 M EUR decrease in heat-production cost achieved by shifting heat extraction to periods of low electricity prices, while the storage investment amounts to 323 k EUR. A comparison to a commonly used simplified storage sizing method, which fails to identify the optimal storage size, demonstrates the advantage of the proposed holistic, physics-based optimization approach in ensuring feasible designs, accurate cost assessments, and optimal storage sizing.

</details>


### [252] [A Local-Linearly Convergent Algorithm for Nonconvex Equality-Constrained Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12665)
*Frank E. Curtis,Lingjun Guo,Daniel P. Robinson*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文扩展了Gradient-Eigenstep算法的分析，证明其在强二阶驻点附近能以局部线性速率收敛（此时退化为对Fletcher增广Lagrangian的梯度下降），并将其作为渐进采样策略的子问题求解器用于等式约束优化，改善了最坏情况下的样本复杂度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 非凸等式约束优化问题需要从任意起点找到近似二阶驻点，现有的Gradient-Eigenstep算法虽然迭代高效，但其收敛速率分析和在大规模采样场景下的应用尚不完善。

Method: 首先证明当算法从强二阶驻点附近初始化、采用足够小的步长参数和足够大的惩罚参数时，算法退化为对Fletcher增广Lagrangian的梯度下降，并可获得局部线性收敛速率；然后将该结果用于渐进采样策略中作为子问题求解器。

Result: 证明了Gradient-Eigenstep算法在合适条件下具有局部线性收敛速率；将其应用于渐进采样策略后，相比直接求解全样本问题，获得了改进的最坏情况样本复杂度。

Conclusion: Gradient-Eigenstep算法不仅具有迭代高效性，还可作为渐进采样框架中的高效子问题求解器，在面向大规模样本均值定义的等式约束优化问题中显著降低样本复杂度。

Abstract: For solving nonconvex equality-constrained optimization problems, a recent Gradient-Eigenstep Algorithm by Goyens et al.~is an iteration-efficient approach, based on minimizing Fletcher's augmented Lagrangian function, for finding an approximate second-order stationary point from an arbitrary starting point. In this paper, the analysis of this algorithm is extended, offering a two-fold contribution. First, it is shown that a local-linear rate of convergence can be obtained by this method if it is initiated sufficiently close to a strong second-order stationary point and employs a sufficiently small step-size parameter and sufficiently large penalty parameter. In this case, the algorithm reduces to a gradient descent algorithm applied to minimize Fletcher's augmented Lagrangian. Second, as a particularly useful application of the first result, it is shown that the Gradient-Eigenstep algorithm can be used as an iteration-efficient subproblem solver in the context of a progressive sampling strategy for solving equality-constrained optimization problems when the objective and constraint functions are defined by large sample averages, ultimately offering an algorithm with an improved worst-case sample complexity when compared to an approach that solves a full-sample problem directly.

</details>


### [253] [Stochastic Mixed-Integer Optimization of Dynamic Electricity Tariffs with Consumer Protection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12688)
*Ananya Kale,Mohit Apte,Chhaya Gosavi*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文基于5,566户伦敦家庭的半小时级用电数据（1.678亿条验证读数），研究了在保持收入接近平价电价基准并限制账单涨幅的约束下，日前居民分时电价设计如何影响削峰效果。通过随机混合整数规划求解，发现在73个测试日内，分段保护随机电价平均削峰2.29%，而取消分段账单上限仅将削峰提升至2.37%，但最差分段账单涨幅从2.48%升至6.81%，表明本样本中实质性平均保护对削峰性能损失极小。同时报告了完整的保护价格前沿，并比较了分段保护与代表性家庭（尾部感知）变体的差异。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 日前居民电价需在需求观测前发布，激进的高价虽能在仿真中削减峰值，但可能推高用户账单。现有研究较少量化"收入中性"和"账单限制"约束对削峰性能的折损，本文旨在回答：当电价设计强制保持收入接近统一费率基准并限制账单增幅时，会损失多少削峰效果。

Method: 使用Low Carbon London项目中5,566户家庭的半小时级数据（167.8百万条验证读数），基于标准电价的对照组采用准实验方法估计价格响应；通过bootstrap生成需求场景；利用HiGHS求解随机混合整数规划（stochastic MILP）；在73个合格留出测试日上评估分段保护型随机电价与无保护变体的表现。

Result: 分段保护随机电价在73个测试日平均削峰2.29%（95%日bootstrap CI [2.11, 2.48]），收入变动-2.26%，最差分段平均账单涨幅2.48%。移除分段账单上限后削峰仅升至2.37%，但最差分段账单涨幅升至6.81%。家庭层面95分位账单涨幅为8.61%（CVaR95为14.76%），说明分段平均上限无法约束家庭尾部风险。代表性家庭（尾部感知）变体将家庭p95从8.61%降至7.05%，而平均削峰仅从2.29%微调至2.28%。

Conclusion: 在本样本中，为保护消费者而施加的平均账单约束对削峰性能的代价很小，但分段层面的保护无法有效约束家庭层面的账单尾部风险。若以家庭尾部风险为目标，代表性家庭（尾部感知）机制可在几乎不损失削峰效果的前提下显著降低最坏家庭账单增幅。本文所有优化结果均为基于选择加入试验的模型反事实，且因批发成本数据不可得，未对利润进行优化。

Abstract: Day-ahead residential tariffs must be posted before demand is observed. Aggressive high prices can cut peaks in simulation, but they can also raise customer bills. This paper measures how much peak reduction is lost when a tariff design problem is required to keep revenue near a flat-tariff baseline and to limit bill increases. Using half-hourly data from 5,566 Low Carbon London households (167.8 million validated readings), we estimate quasi-experimental price response against the standard-tariff comparison group, form bootstrap demand scenarios, and solve stochastic mixed-integer programs with HiGHS. On all 73 eligible held-out test days, a segment-protected stochastic tariff reduces simulated peak demand by 2.29% on average (95% day-bootstrap CI [2.11, 2.48]), with revenue change -2.26% and mean worst-segment bill increase 2.48%. Removing the segment bill cap raises peak reduction only to 2.37%, while the worst segment bill increase rises to 6.81%: in this sample, substantial average protection costs little peak-shaving performance. The same schedules leave a household 95th-percentile bill increase of 8.61% (CVaR95 14.76%), so segment-average caps do not bound household tails. We report the full price-of-protection frontier under consistent household simulation and compare segment protection with a representative-household (tail-aware) variant: the latter cuts household p95 from 8.61% to 7.05% while changing mean peak reduction only from 2.29% to 2.28%. All optimized outcomes are model-based counterfactuals under an opt-in trial; wholesale costs are unavailable, so we do not optimize profit.

</details>


### [254] [An Algebraic Method for Optimizing the State, Control, and Terminal State Weight Matrices for Optimal Feedback Control](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12702)
*Daegyun Choi,Donghoon Kim,James D. Turner*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种利用闭环解析解（而非数值积分）来优化最优反馈控制算法中权重矩阵的方法，通过解析梯度最小化终端状态值，从而大幅降低传统方法中权重矩阵调优的计算成本。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 最优反馈控制算法中的性能指标包含状态惩罚、控制惩罚和终端状态惩罚等自由参数矩阵，这些权重矩阵的选择通常是实验性和迭代性的，导致计算开销大且效率低。传统上，权重矩阵的优化过程需要多次数值积分，计算成本高昂，亟需更高效的求解方法。

Method: 本文提出一种优化过程来生成最优反馈控制算法的权重矩阵。核心方法包括：(1) 利用时变Riccati矩阵、状态轨迹、状态转移矩阵和最优性能指标的闭环（closed-form）解析解，替代传统的数值积分过程；(2) 使用闭环代数方程计算所有偏导数，从而生成用于优化步骤的解析梯度；(3) 优化策略以最小化反馈控制问题的终端状态值为目标。

Result: 通过一个数值示例验证了所提出优化算法的有效性。该方法完全避免了数值积分，使得权重矩阵的优化过程在计算效率上得到显著提升。

Conclusion: 所提出的计算流程预期可广泛应用于科学与工程中的控制理论应用，为最优反馈控制权重矩阵的自动调优提供了高效且通用的解决方案。

Abstract: The necessary conditions for formulating optimal feedback control algorithms have been known for many years. Free parameters exist in the performance index in the form of state and control penalty and terminal state penalty matrices for tuning the performance of the optimally controlled system. The selection process is typically experimental and iterative. To generate the weight matrices for optimal feedback control algorithms, an optimization process is proposed. Typically, the optimization process for the weight matrices requires several numerical integration processes that are computationally expensive; this work overcomes the classical high computational cost by exploiting closed-form solutions for the time-varying Riccati matrix, state trajectories, state transition matrix, and the optimal performance index. Closed-form algebraic equations are used to generate all partial derivative calculations, and no numerical integration is required. The closed-form partial derivatives are used to generate analytic gradients for the optimization steps. The optimization strategy seeks to minimize the terminal state values for the feedback control problem. A numerical example is presented to demonstrate the effectiveness of the proposed optimization algorithm. The resulting computational procedures are expected to be broadly useful for control theory applications in science and engineering.

</details>


### [255] [Efficient Hessian-Free Methods for Multi-Objective Bilevel Optimization with Nonconvex Lower Level](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12704)
*Yicong Jiang,Feihu Huang*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文提出了一类基于Moreau包络的无Hessian算法MOMEHA及其动量变体MB-MOMEHA，用于求解下层级问题非凸的多目标双层学习问题，并给出了确定性与随机设定下的收敛性保证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有针对多目标双层优化的方法大多依赖下层问题的（强）凸性假设，而实际的多目标双层学习问题中下层问题通常是非凸的。这一假设限制严重阻碍了方法在自动化学习、多任务元学习等实际AI场景中的应用。

Method: 利用Moreau包络将原始多目标双层问题转化为带包络约束的多目标单层优化问题；通过平滑加权Tchebycheff标量化方法，在设计上保持单循环（single-loop）且无需Hessian计算的优点；进一步提出基于动量的变体MB-MOMEHA用于随机情形。

Result: 在确定性与随机两种设定下均提供了收敛性分析；在少样本元学习和神经架构搜索实验中，所提方法在Pareto前沿上优于现有方法，验证了其有效性和鲁棒性。

Conclusion: MOMEHA及其动量变体填补了非凸下层多目标双层优化方法的空白，兼顾了计算效率（单循环、无Hessian）与理论保证，并在实际任务中展现出优越性能。

Abstract: Multi-objective bilevel optimization has wide applications in the AI area such as automated learning and multi-task meta-learning. Although recently some works have been begun to study the multi-objective bilevel optimization, the proposed methods rely on the (strongly) convex lower level problems. In fact, these multi-objective bilevel learning problems are generally nonconvex, and particularly their lower level problems are nonconvex. To fill this gap, we propose a class of Multi-Objective Moreau Envelope based Hessian-free Algorithms (MOMEHA) to solve the multi-objective bilevel learning problems with nonconvex lower level. Specifically, our method uses the Moreau envelope to convert the original problem into a multi-objective single-level optimization with an envelope constraint. In particular, our method retains computational advantages of being single-loop and Hessian-free in the multi-objective setting by incorporating a smooth weighted Tchebycheff scalarization. Furthermore, we propose a momentum-based variant of MOMEHA (i.e., MB-MOMEHA) method to solve the stochastic multi-objective bilevel learning problems. In theory, we provide the convergence properties of our algorithms under both deterministic and stochastic setting. Some experiments on few-shot meta-learning and neural architecture search demonstrate that our methods outperform the existing approaches in Pareto front, validating its effectiveness and robustness.

</details>


### [256] [Shape Design for Degenerate Hyperbolic Equation with Degenerate Boundary and Its Application to Observability](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12754)
*Dong-Hui Yang,Yuanzhi Zhou*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文研究了一类退化双曲方程的可观测性与可控性问题，其中控制区域与退化集相交。与已有研究主要处理控制区域远离退化点的情况不同，本文首次考虑了控制区域触及退化部分的情形。通过引入基于形状设计的近似方法，将退化方程近似为一系列一致双曲方程，利用谱近似、弱解的精确估计和乘子方法，建立了逼近方程的可观测性不等式（常数与近似参数无关），并通过退化极限得到原始退化方程的可观测性不等式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 已有关于退化双曲方程可观测性与可控性的研究主要聚焦于控制区域与退化集分离的情形，而控制区域触及退化部分的情况尚未被充分解决。由于退化性带来的技术困难，需要发展新的数学工具来克服这一挑战。

Method: (1) 引入基于形状设计的近似方法，将退化方程用一族一致双曲方程逼近；(2) 利用相关算子的谱逼近技术；(3) 对弱解建立精确估计；(4) 采用乘子方法；(5) 先对逼近方程建立可观测性不等式，确保常数与近似参数无关，再通过退化极限过渡到原始方程。

Result: 建立了逼近方程的可观测性不等式，其中的常数与近似参数无关；通过退化极限过程，得到了原始退化方程的可观测性不等式，进而可推出相应的可控性结论。

Conclusion: 本文成功填补了退化双曲方程控制理论中控制区域触及退化集这一情形的空白，所提出的形状设计近似方法为处理类似退化问题提供了新的技术途径。

Abstract: In this paper, we study the observability and controllability of a class of degenerate hyperbolic equations with a control region intersecting the degenerate set. Unlike the existing results that mainly deal with control regions separated from the degeneracy, we consider the case where the control region reaches the degenerate part. To handle the difficulty caused by the degeneracy, we introduce a shape-design-based approximation method based on shape design by approximating the degenerate equation with a family of uniformly hyperbolic equations. The proof relies on the spectral approximation of the associated operators, precise estimates for weak solutions, and the multiplier method. We first establish observability inequalities for the approximating equations with constants independent of the approximation parameter. Then, by passing to the degenerate limit, we obtain the observability inequality for the original degenerate equation.

</details>


### [257] [Difference-of-Convex Regularization for Graph Learning by Differentiable Programming](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12757)
*Liping Tao,Chee Wei Tan*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文提出一种Difference-of-Convex Regularizer (DCR)图学习框架，通过正则化最大似然估计（MLE）和对偶表示近似拉普拉斯伪逆的谱作用，无需直接求逆即可高效求解拉普拉斯正则化非负最小二乘（LR-NNLS）问题，并提供了稳定性和唯一不动点的理论保证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 拉普拉斯正则化最小化是信号处理和机器学习中的基础工具，但受限于图拉普拉斯伪逆的稠密性和病态性。拉普拉斯矩阵本身是稀疏的，但其伪逆是稠密且通常病态的，导致大规模场景下直接计算不可行，且伪逆学习比拉普拉斯学习更具挑战性。因此，需要一个无需直接求逆即可近似伪逆谱作用的高效框架。

Method: 本文在给定图拉普拉斯的设定下，提出Difference-of-Convex Regularizer (DCR)图学习框架。具体地：(1) 通过正则化最大似然估计（MLE）近似拉普拉斯伪逆的谱作用，避免直接求逆；(2) 通过对偶表示重新表述LR-NNLS问题，将伪逆学习与实例特定推理解耦；(3) 通过可微的对偶引导学习方案实现高效的原问题解重构；(4) 建立算法稳定性与唯一不动点存在的理论保证。

Result: 数值实验表明，DCR框架在多样化的图拓扑结构上相比凸求解器和图滤波基线方法取得了更优的性能，并展现出稳健的表现。

Conclusion: DCR框架有效解决了拉普拉斯伪逆稠密和病态带来的计算挑战，通过解耦伪逆学习与实例推理、利用对偶表示和可微学习方案，在理论上保证了稳定性与不动点唯一性，实践上优于现有凸求解器和图滤波方法。

Abstract: Laplacian-regularized minimization is fundamental in signal processing and machine learning, but is limited by the dense and ill-conditioned nature of the graph Laplacian pseudoinverse. While the Laplacian itself is sparse, its pseudoinverse is dense and often ill-conditioned, rendering direct computation impractical at scale. Moreover, pseudoinverse learning is more challenging than Laplacian learning. To address this challenge, this paper considers the setting where the graph Laplacian is given and proposes a Difference-of-Convex Regularizer (DCR) graph learning framework that approximates the spectral action of the Laplacian pseudoinverse without direct inversion via regularized Maximum Likelihood Estimation (MLE). By reformulating Laplacian-Regularized Nonnegative Least Squares (LR-NNLS) through a dual representation, DCR decouples pseudoinverse learning from instance-specific inference and enables efficient primal solution reconstruction via a differentiable dual-guided learning scheme. We establish theoretical guarantees on stability and the existence of a unique fixed point for DCR algorithm. Numerical experiments demonstrate improved performance over convex solvers and graph filtering baselines and robust performance across diverse graph topologies.

</details>


### [258] [A penalty-type method for relaxed inverse optimal control problems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12769)
*Floriane Mefo Kue,Patrick Mehlitz,Thorsten Raasch*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文提出并分析了一种用于求解由逆最优控制引出的双层优化问题的惩罚型方法。该方法通过求解一系列代理问题来逼近松弛值函数重构的驻点，具有上下层变量解耦更新、每迭代仅需一次下层问题评估、无需线搜索且罚参数无需趋于无穷等优点，在合理假设下获得子序列收敛结果，并通过数值实验验证了有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 逆最优控制问题可建模为一类双层优化问题，但直接求解此类问题在数值上具有挑战性。现有方法往往需要线搜索、多次下层问题求解或使罚参数趋于无穷，计算代价高昂。因此需要一种更高效、更稳健的数值方法来解决这一特定类别的双层优化问题。

Method: 该方法采用惩罚型策略处理相关的松弛值函数重构。具体而言：1) 通过一系列代理问题逼近原问题的驻点，将松弛值函数约束进行惩罚；2) 上层与下层决策变量的更新被解耦；3) 罚参数仅在迭代未带来充分可行性度量改进时才增大；4) 不包含任何线搜索步骤，每迭代仅需评估一次下层问题。

Result: 在合理假设下获得了子序列收敛结果；数值实验中同时还驱动松弛参数趋于零，可视化结果表明该方法具有有效性。

Conclusion: 所提出的惩罚型方法为逆最优控制中的双层优化问题提供了一种无需线搜索、计算开销低的求解方案，并具有理论收敛保证和数值验证，展示了该方法的实用性与有效性。

Abstract: This paper is devoted to the introduction and analysis
  of a penalty-type method for the numerical treatment of
  a class of bilevel optimization problems arising from inverse optimal control.
  The algorithm is designed to compute stationary points of the associated relaxed value function reformulation.
  This is achieved by determining a sequence of stationary points associated with a sequence of surrogate problems
  where the relaxed value function constraint is penalized,
  where the updates of upper- and lower-level decision variables are decoupled,
  and where the penalty parameter is enlarged only in those iterations
  which do not come along with a sufficient improvement of some feasibility measure.
  The resulting method does not comprise any linesearch, the lower-level problem has to be evaluated just once per iteration,
  and the penalty parameter does not need to be driven to infinity.
  Nevertheless, subsequential convergence results are obtained under reasonable assumptions.
  Numerical experiments, where the relaxation parameter is also driven to zero, visualize effectiveness of the approach.

</details>


### [259] [Mean Field Stackelberg Game for Production and Carbon Emission Reduction with State Reflections](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12775)
*Lijun Bo,Zhen Liu,Jingfei Wang,Jiacheng Zhang*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文构建了一个含均值场交互和公共噪声的两层Stackelberg博弈模型，用于研究全球变暖背景下中央管理者（领导者）通过产品价格引导n个异质竞争地区（追随者）减排的问题，并利用反射机制（局部时间过程）对排放硬上限进行建模，证明了近似Stackelberg均衡的存在性并进行了蒙特卡洛敏感性分析。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 全球变暖由人为碳排放驱动，具有跨界污染特征且损害不可逆，对人类构成生存威胁。现有模型难以同时刻画分层决策、层级间战略互动、排放上限的硬约束以及区域间竞争的均值场交互效应。

Method: 构建两层的Stackelberg均值场博弈：领导者（中央监管者）通过调整产品价格进行引导；追随者（n个异质地区）在状态反射的排放动力学下相互竞争。采用含公共噪声的均值场交互控制刻画区域间耦合，并通过局部时间过程（反射机制）建模紧急减排以实现排放硬上限约束。理论方面证明近似Stackelberg均衡的存在性，数值方面采用蒙特卡洛模拟进行敏感性分析。

Result: 建立了近似Stackelberg均衡的存在性定理，并通过蒙特卡洛模拟对模型关键参数进行了敏感性分析，验证了模型在刻画分层减排决策中的有效性。

Conclusion: 该模型为全球气候治理提供了一种兼具层级决策、区域异质性、跨界交互和硬排放约束的数理框架，可用于分析碳定价等政策工具在竞争性区域间的传导效应。

Abstract: Global warming, driven by anthropogenic carbon emissions with transboundary pollution characteristics and irreversible damage, poses an existential threat to human society. This paper develops a novel two-level Stackelberg game with mean field interaction of controls and common noise which integrates hierarchical decision-making under a state-reflected emission dynamics. A central regulator (leader) adjusts product prices to guide $n$ heterogeneous competing regions (followers) while enforcing a hard emission cap via a reflection mechanism that models emergency reductions through a local time process. We establish the existence of an approximate Stackelberg equilibrium and perform sensitivity analysis via Monte Carlo simulations.

</details>


### [260] [Feedback Diagonalization and Stabilization for Discrete Linear Ensemble Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12813)
*Jonathan M. Bosnich,Xudong Chen*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文研究一类由无穷多个线性标量控制系统组成的离散线性集合系统（ensemble system），所有子系统由共同标量控制输入驱动。文章给出了存在静态线性反馈控制律，使得闭环集合系统满足稳定性充分必要条件的刻画，证明序列 (|b_n|) 和 (a_n/|b_n|) 的指数衰减是必要的，并在关于期望极点的假设下充分。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 与有限维线性系统不同，无穷维线性系统的极点位于开左半平面并不足以保证其稳定性。稳定性仅在极点在闭左半平面且无穷小生成元可对角化时得到保证。因此需要刻画何种条件下存在公共反馈控制使得闭环集合系统满足这两个条件，即系统可反馈镇定。

Method: 通过分析集合系统的无穷小生成元的谱性质和对角化条件，将反馈镇定问题转化为对序列 (|b_n|) 和 (a_n/|b_n|) 的增长/衰减性质的刻画。利用指数衰减条件建立静态线性反馈控制律存在的必要与充分条件。

Result: 证明了序列 (|b_n|)_{n∈N} 和 (a_n/|b_n|)_{n∈N} 的指数衰减是存在静态线性反馈控制律使闭环集合系统满足稳定性条件（极点在闭左半平面且生成元可对角化）的必要条件；在关于期望极点位置的一定假设下，该指数衰减条件也是充分的。

Conclusion: 离散线性集合系统的反馈可镇定性与系统参数序列的指数衰减性密切相关。指数衰减条件在适当的期望极点假设下，构成了静态线性反馈镇定存在的充要条件。

Abstract: We consider a countably infinite collection of linear, scalar control systems, where each system is represented by the pair $(a_n,b_n)$ with $a_n>0$ for $n\in \mathbb{N}$, and all systems are forced by a common scalar control input. We refer to this collection of systems as a discrete linear ensemble system. The ensemble system is feedback stabilizable if there exists a common feedback control input that asymptotically stabilizes every system simultaneously. Unlike finite-dimensional linear systems, an infinite-dimensional linear system is not guaranteed to be stable if its poles lie in the open left half-plane. Stability is guaranteed, however, if (i) its poles are contained in the closed left half-plane and (ii) its infinitesimal generator is diagonalizable. In this paper, we provide necessary and sufficient conditions for the existence of a static, linear feedback control law that ensures the closed-loop ensemble system satisfies (i) and (ii). In particular, we show that the exponential decay of the sequences $(|b_n|)_{n\in \mathbb{N}}$ and $(a_n/|b_n|)_{n\in \mathbb{N}}$ is necessary and, under an assumption on the desired poles, sufficient.

</details>


### [261] [A Lebeau--Robbiano approach to the controllability of the Boussinesq system with a reduced number of controls](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12815)
*Víctor Hernández-Santamaría*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文证明了二维和三维Boussinesq系统在减少控制数量（仅控制温度方程和N-2个速度分量）条件下的局部零能控性，采用Lebeau-Robbiano谱方法，关键创新在于提出混合可观性估计（仅对Stokes分量进行谱局部化，热分量无需频率限制），并利用线性化系统的级联结构克服了Stokes算子与Dirichlet拉普拉斯算子谱分解不一致的困难，最终控制代价满足C exp(C/T)的抛物线阶。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Boussinesq系统作为描述流体热对流的经典模型，其控制问题具有重要意义。以往结果通常需要较多控制或施加更严格的频谱条件。本文旨在在减少控制数量的约束下，证明Boussinesq系统的局部零能控性，即只用温度方程和一个速度分量（二维中）或两个速度分量（三维中）的控制，即可实现能控。

Method: 采用Lebeau-Robbiano谱方法。具体而言：(1) 利用线性化系统的级联结构（速度方程与温度方程耦合），克服Stokes算子和Dirichlet拉普拉斯算子谱分解不匹配的困难；(2) 构建混合可观性估计——仅对Stokes分量做谱局部化，热分量不施加任何频率限制；(3) 结合高Stokes频率的耗散性质，对线性化系统执行Lebeau-Robbiano迭代；(4) 通过时间迭代（不动点）论证将线性控制估计推广到非线性Boussinesq系统。

Result: 证明了二维和三维Boussinesq系统在减少控制数量条件下的局部零能控性；控制代价满足小时间边界C exp(C/T)，恢复了期望的抛物线阶，同时保持控制数量的减少。

Conclusion: 通过巧妙利用系统的级联耦合结构和混合谱可观性估计，在减少控制数量的前提下成功建立了Boussinesq系统的局部零能控性，且控制代价符合抛物线特性，为相关流体控制问题提供了新的理论工具。

Abstract: In this paper, we prove the local null controllability of the Boussinesq system in dimensions two and three with a reduced number of localized controls. More precisely, the controls act on the temperature equation and on only $N-2$ components of the velocity equation. Our proof is based on a spectral approach in the spirit of the Lebeau--Robbiano method.
  The main difficulty comes from the coupled structure of the system. The velocity and temperature equations are governed by the Stokes operator and the Dirichlet Laplacian, respectively. Their natural spectral decompositions are different, and there is no common spectral localization naturally adapted to the coupled system. Therefore, the usual componentwise spectral argument cannot be applied directly. We show that the cascade structure of the linearized system makes it possible to overcome this difficulty. The main ingredient is a mixed observability estimate in which only the Stokes component is spectrally localized, while the heat component is treated without any frequency restriction. Combining this estimate with the dissipation of the high Stokes frequencies allows us to carry out a Lebeau--Robbiano iteration for the linearized system. The resulting linear controllability estimate is transferred to the nonlinear Boussinesq system through a time-iteration argument. As an important consequence, the controls have a small-time cost bounded by $C\exp(C/T)$, which recovers the expected parabolic order while preserving the reduced number of controls.

</details>


### [262] [Distribution Steering via Sliced Optimal Transport Control](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12828)
*Kaito Ito,Anqi Dong*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于切片最优传输的有限时域控制框架，通过单方向投影的最优传输映射设计随机化单方向控制器，并通过对投影方向的平均得到确定性切片反馈律，用于驱动动力系统的状态分布从初始分布到目标分布。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 分布操控旨在设计反馈律将动力系统的状态分布从给定的初始分布驱动至终端分布。最优传输提供了自然的几何方法，但传统做法通常需要在全状态空间中构造传输映射或耦合，计算代价高且难以直接转化为可实现的控制律。

Method: 提出基于切片最优传输的有限时域控制框架：在每个采样时刻，通过投影最优传输映射定义方向性终端条件，并使用最小能量实现得到随机化单方向控制器；对投影方向取平均得到确定性切片反馈。针对单积分器动力学证明切片Wasserstein距离非增；对高斯端点分布，反馈是仿射的且保持高斯性。进一步识别与分布相关的增益以实现线性衰减，并给出控制能量的显式刻画。最后扩展至线性动力学系统，利用可达性归一化坐标和局部可控性Gramian实现。

Result: 单积分器动力学下，平均反馈使切片Wasserstein距离到目标非增；高斯端点分布下反馈为仿射且保持高斯性，均值和协方差可精确驱动至指定终端值；识别出可产生切片Wasserstein距离线性衰减的分布相关增益及能量显式刻画；随机化控制器在采样周期趋于零时收敛至平均切片流；扩展到线性系统后通过可达性归一化坐标和可控性Gramian实现精确有限步实现。

Conclusion: 该框架成功将切片最优传输从纯粹的几何距离度量扩展为可实现的控制律设计工具，避免了全状态空间传输映射的构建，为分布驱动问题提供了计算可行且具有理论保证的解决方案。

Abstract: Distribution steering seeks feedback laws that drive the state law of a dynamical system between prescribed initial and terminal distributions. Optimal transport provides a natural geometric approach, but its implementation generally requires a transport map or coupling in the full state space. Sliced optimal transport avoids this full-dimensional construction through one-dimensional projections. Yet, the resulting projected maps specify only directional displacements and do not by themselves prescribe a realizable feedback law. To this end, we develop a finite-horizon control framework based on sliced optimal transport. At each sampling instant, a projected optimal transport map defines a directional terminal condition, whose minimum-energy realization yields a randomized single-direction controller. Averaging over projection directions gives a deterministic sliced feedback. For the single-integrator dynamics, the averaged feedback makes the sliced Wasserstein distance to the target non-increasing. For Gaussian endpoint laws, it is affine, preserves Gaussianity, and steers the mean and covariance to their prescribed terminal values. We further identify a law-dependent gain that yields linear decay of the sliced Wasserstein distance together with an explicit characterization of the control energy. We also prove that the randomized controller converges to the averaged sliced flow as the sampling period vanishes. Finally, we extend the construction to linear dynamical systems. Reachability-normalized coordinates allow instantaneous realization of the sliced velocity for uniformly fully actuated systems, while local controllability Gramians provide exact finite-step realization for general controllable systems. Numerical examples illustrate the resulting distributional flows.

</details>


### [263] [Distance-Based Formation Control with Prescribed Performance for Higher-Order Multi-Agent Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12896)
*Janina Schaa,Maria Charitidou,Dimos Dimarogonas,Thomas Berger*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文研究具有规定瞬态性能的分布式基于距离的编队控制，适用于相对阶大于1的未知非线性多智能体系统，提出了无模型分布式控制律，并验证了漏斗约束下编队与跟踪误差的满足性以及闭环信号的有界性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有分布式基于距离的编队控制大多假设系统具有相对阶为一的动力学，或需要精确的模型知识，难以适用于实际中普遍存在的具有更高相对阶和未知非线性的多智能体系统。本文旨在突破这一限制，同时保证编队瞬态性能的预定约束。

Method: 引入虚拟领导者，其轨迹由指定智能体子集按规定瞬态行为跟踪。通信图采用树图或最小且无穷小刚性图，后者还考虑了质心跟踪目标。针对每种情况，设计了分布式且无模型的（model-free）控制律，利用漏斗控制框架确保形成误差和跟踪误差满足预定约束。

Result: 建立了编队误差与跟踪误差满足既定漏斗约束的理论保证，并证明了所有闭环信号有界。数值仿真验证了所提控制律的有效性。

Conclusion: 该工作将距离编队控制拓展到相对阶大于一的未知非线性系统，通过无模型分布式控制实现了预定的瞬态性能，兼顾了树图和刚性图两类通信拓扑，并支持质心跟踪，具有较广的适用性。

Abstract: We consider distributed distance-based formation control with prescribed transient performance for multi-agent systems modeled by unknown nonlinear dynamics of relative degree greater than one. We introduce a virtual leader whose trajectory is tracked with prescribed transient behavior by a designated subset of agents. The undirected communication graph is either a tree graph or a minimally and infinitesimally rigid graph. In the latter case, we further consider the objective of centroid tracking. In each setting, a distributed and model-free control law is developed, and we establish the satisfaction of the prescribed funnel constraints on the formation and tracking errors, and boundedness of all closed-loop signals. Numerical simulations illustrate the effectiveness of the proposed control laws.

</details>


### [264] [Optimizing Multi-Stakeholder Fairness in Computed Tomography Appointment Scheduling with Predicted Scan and Reporting Duration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13059)
*Ludovico Ambrosi,Chandra Bortolotto,Sara Cambiaghi,Luisa Carone,Davide Duma,Lorenzo Preda*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文提出一种预测-处方式框架，用于公平感知的随访CT调度。结合患者特异性机器学习模型预测检查和报告时长，并将其嵌入多目标混合整数线性规划模型中，同时最小化患者首选检查日期的偏差，并通过字典序极小极大公平准则平衡放射科医生的报告工作量。基于真实急诊放射科数据的实验表明，仅允许1-2天的调度灵活性即可大幅改善工作公平性；提出的支配性约简策略在不影响Pareto前沿的情况下消除了大部分ε-约束评估。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有随访CT调度方法仅优化扫描仪利用率或患者等待时间，忽视了报告活动以及多利益相关者间的公平性平衡。需要一种同时兼顾患者时间偏好和放射科医生工作量均衡的调度方案。

Method: 采用预测-处方式框架：首先训练患者特异性ML模型（如XGBoost）预测检查和报告时长；然后将预测嵌入多目标MILP模型，通过字典序极小极大公平准则平衡放射科医生工作量，并在ε-约束框架内推导支配性约简属性以减少Pareto前沿求解的计算量。

Result: 基于真实急诊放射科数据的实验表明：仅1-2天的调度灵活性即可显著改善工作量公平性并保持患者及时访问；支配性约简策略在不影响Pareto前沿的情况下消除了大部分ε-约束评估；通过下游优化遗憾评估，XGBoost在调度决策支持上优于传统预测误差指标表现更好的模型。

Conclusion: 公平感知的随访CT调度可以通过小幅牺牲患者时间偏好换取显著的工作量公平性提升；决策感知的模型评估（优化遗憾）比传统预测指标更适合选择预测模型；支配性约简策略有效提升了多目标优化的计算效率。

Abstract: Scheduling follow-up Computed Tomography (CT) examinations requires balancing two competing objectives: assigning patients as close as possible to their recommended examination dates while ensuring an equitable distribution of radiologists' workload. Existing approaches optimize scanner utilization or patient waiting times, overlooking reporting activities and the need to balance fairness across multiple stakeholders. This paper proposes a predictive-prescriptive framework for fairness-aware follow-up CT scheduling. Patient-specific Machine Learning (ML) models are first developed to predict both examination and reporting durations. These predictions are then embedded into a multi-objective Mixed-Integer Linear Programming (MILP) model that simultaneously minimizes deviations from patients' preferred examination dates and balances radiologists' reporting workloads through a lexicographic min-max fairness criterion. We derive a dominance reduction property within an $ε$-constraint framework that substantially reduces the number of optimization problems required to generate the Pareto frontier. Computational experiments based on data from a real-world emergency radiology department show that allowing patients a scheduling flexibility of only one to two days is sufficient to substantially improve workload equity among radiologists while preserving timely access to follow-up examinations. The proposed dominance reduction strategy eliminates most $ε$-constraint evaluations without affecting the Pareto frontier. Finally, evaluating predictive models through downstream optimization regret demonstrates that XGBoost provides the most effective support for scheduling decisions, outperforming models that achieve lower prediction errors according to conventional predictive metrics.

</details>


### [265] [Adaptive Schauder Stochastic Mirror Descent in Banach Spaces](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13121)
*Jinhui Bai,Shuai Lu,Lei Shi*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文将随机镜像下降（SMD）扩展到无限维Banach空间，用于求解一类风险泛函最小化问题。通过构造基于熵函数的Bregman距离，并利用Schauder基引入随样本量自适应的有限维子空间正则化策略，算法实现了O(n^{1+θ})时间和O(n^θ)内存的复杂度，收敛率达到O(n^{-1/p_1})（p_1≥2），并进一步推广到不适定逆问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的随机镜像下降方法主要局限于有限维或特定类型的空间，而许多实际问题（如反问题、风险泛函最小化）需要在无限维Banach空间中处理。将SMD扩展到无限维空间面临Bregman距离构造、子空间逼近和随机梯度投影等挑战，本文旨在解决这些问题。

Method: 首先根据Banach空间的均匀凸性选择合适的Bregman距离；对于非均匀凸空间L^1_{ρ_X}(Ω)，构造基于熵函数的Bregman距离。基于Schauder基引入随样本量n自适应的有限维子空间族，在每次SMD迭代中将子问题限制在相应有限维子空间上，并将随机梯度投影到对应的有限维对偶空间，形成新的正则化策略，实现偏差-方差权衡。

Result: 算法时间复杂度为O(n^{1+θ})，空间复杂度为O(n^θ)，其中θ>0在最小化器具有足够正则性时可任意小。通过新的分析框架证明了收敛率为O(n^{-1/p_1})（至多差对数因子），其中p_1≥2由底空间凸性决定。在最小化器仅满足较弱正则性的misspecified设定下，算法仍收敛到最小值。数值实验验证了算法的有效性。

Conclusion: 本文成功将随机镜像下降推广到无限维Banach空间，通过巧妙的有限维子空间正则化策略兼顾了计算效率和收敛性能，算法具有良好的理论和实践效果，并已扩展到逆问题领域。

Abstract: In this paper, we extend stochastic mirror descent (SMD) to infinite-dimensional Banach spaces for solving a class of risk functional minimization problems, where stochastic gradient information is only available through sampling. We first choose the Bregman distance according to the uniform convexity properties of the Banach space. For the non-uniformly convex space $\mathcal{L}_{ρ_X}^1(Ω)$, we instead construct a Bregman distance induced by the entropy function. Based on a Schauder basis of the Banach space, we introduce a family of finite-dimensional subspaces that adapt to the sample size $n$. At each SMD iteration, we restrict the subproblem to the corresponding finite-dimensional subspace and project the stochastic gradient onto the associated finite-dimensional dual space, thereby introducing a new regularization strategy. This regularization strategy allows us to explicitly solve the SMD subproblem efficiently and to achieve a bias--variance trade-off. The algorithm requires $\mathcal{O}(n^{1+θ})$ time and $\mathcal{O}(n^θ)$ memory, where $θ>0$ can be chosen arbitrarily small when the minimizer has sufficient regularity. By developing a new analytical framework, we prove that the proposed algorithm achieves a convergence rate of $\mathcal{O}\left(n^{-1/p_1}\right)$ up to logarithmic factors, where $p_1\geq2$ is determined by the convexity properties of the underlying space. In the misspecified setting where the minimizer satisfies only weaker regularity conditions, we show that the proposed algorithm still converges to the minimum. We further extend the algorithm to inverse problems and validate its effectiveness in numerical experiments.

</details>


### [266] [Positive stabilization of a pure diffusion system](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13203)
*Violaine Piengeon,Joseph J. Winkin*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文对具有点观测的一类边界控制扩散系统,完整解析地研究了正镇定、正状态估计以及基于观测器的正镇定问题,并在连续和离散两种设定下给出了显式、可直接实现的解。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 针对边界控制扩散系统,以往的镇定和估计研究往往缺乏对"正性"(即系统状态和控制器保持非负性)的完整解析处理,且观测器设计与反馈镇定相结合的原始策略尚属空白。本文旨在填补这一理论空缺,同时兼顾数值实现与理论解之间的严格对比。

Method: 采用完整的解析方法求解三个核心问题:(1)正镇定问题;(2)正状态估计问题;(3)基于观测器的正镇定问题。对连续PDE系统推导出最优设计的解析解,并提出一种原创的观测器反馈镇定策略;同时将研究推广到任意阶空间离散系统,实现数值求解并与连续名义解进行严格对比。

Result: 在连续与离散两种设定下,均获得了显式且易于实现的设计解;最优设计问题得到解析求解;所提出的观测器反馈镇定策略被验证有效;离散化方案与名义PDE解之间的对比结果具有一致性。

Conclusion: 本文为边界控制扩散系统的正镇定、正估计及观测器反馈镇定提供了完整的解析理论框架,所得显式解具备良好的可实现性,且在离散化下性能与连续解一致,为该类系统的工程实现奠定了理论基础。

Abstract: This paper provides a complete analytical study of the positive stabilization, positive state estimation, and observer-based positive stabilization problems for a generic boundary control diffusion system with point observation. In particular, optimal design problems are solved analytically, and an original strategy for observer-based feedback stabilization is proposed. The three problems are also investigated for an arbitrary-order spatial discretization of the nominal PDE system enabling numerical implementation together with rigorous comparisons with the nominal solutions. In both the continuous and discretized settings, explicit and readily implementable solutions are derived.

</details>


### [267] [A stabilized sequential quadratic programming method for degenerate nonlinear optimization problems on Riemannian manifolds](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13298)
*Yuya Yamakawa,Mamoru Oka*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种用于黎曼流形上退化约束优化问题的稳定序列二次规划（SQP）方法。在不假设任何约束规格（constraint qualification）的前提下，证明了生成的序列存在累积点，该累积点是KKT点、近似KKT（AKKT）点或相关可行性问题的稳定点，并通过数值实验验证了该方法对退化问题的有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统的黎曼SQP方法仅在约束规格成立时才能保证全局收敛，但对于退化约束优化问题（即约束规格可能失效的情形），现有方法的收敛行为无法得到保证。因此需要将欧几里得空间中的稳定SQP框架推广到黎曼流形，以处理退化问题。

Method: 将稳定SQP方法从欧几里得空间扩展到黎曼流形。该方法不依赖任何约束规格假设，通过求解稳定化的二次子问题来处理退化情形，并利用流形上的几何结构（如切空间、指数映射等）进行迭代更新。

Result: 在不假设约束规格的情况下，证明了生成的序列存在累积点，该累积点属于以下三种类型之一：KKT点、近似KKT（AKKT）点、或相关可行性问题的稳定点。数值实验证实了该方法对退化问题的有效性。

Conclusion: 本文成功将稳定SQP框架从欧几里得空间推广到黎曼流形，为退化约束优化问题提供了一种无需约束规格假设即可保证收敛的求解方法，弥补了现有黎曼SQP方法在退化情形下的理论空白。

Abstract: We propose a stabilized sequential quadratic programming (SQP) method for degenerate constrained optimization problems on Riemannian manifolds. The problem considered in this study is a Riemannian nonlinear programming problem (RNLP) with equality and inequality constraints, where classical constraint qualifications may fail. While existing Riemannian SQP methods guarantee global convergence only under constraint qualifications, their convergence behavior is not ensured for degenerate problems. To address this limitation, we extend the stabilized SQP framework from Euclidean spaces to Riemannian manifolds. Without assuming any constraint qualification, we prove that the generated sequence has an accumulation point that is a Karush--Kuhn--Tucker (KKT) point, an approximate KKT (AKKT) point, or a stationary point of an associated feasibility problem. Finally, we conduct numerical experiments to confirm the effectiveness of the proposed method for degenerate problems.

</details>


### [268] [Distributed and Dynamic Hub Network Operation Planning in a Hyperconnected Less-Than-Truckload Operating System](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13419)
*Tiankuo Zhang,Jihye Jung,Paria Nourmohammadi,Benoit Montreuil,Alan Erera,Sahrish Jaleel Shaikh*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文研究基于物理互联网（PI）原则的零担（LTL）物流多枢纽运营规划协议，通过模块化集装箱和开放资源共享，设计分布式协议以协调多区域枢纽网络间的合作，并通过仿真实验验证其有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LTL行业对物流系统效率和可持续性至关重要，但由于运营效率低下和中小承运商网络高度碎片化，LTL运输的平均成本显著偏高。现有系统缺乏跨枢纽网络的分布式协调机制，需要设计灵活动态的多枢纽运营协议来提升整体效率。

Method: 基于Physical Internet（PI）原则构建分布式动态物流枢纽网络系统，针对每个枢纽网络定义货物的预期到达时间和最晚离开时间，设计一套多枢纽运营规划协议（包括hub内货物分配到指定目的地拖车和hub间拖车发车调度），同时精心设计枢纽网络间的互联机制以实现合作。

Result: 通过多场景仿真实验评估所提出协议在运营商多枢纽网络中的有效性，结果表明该协议能够实现合作成果并满足货物请求，提升了PI网络的实用性和鲁棒性。

Conclusion: 该研究增强了基于PI的物流网络的实用性和鲁棒性，促进了枢纽网络间更大程度的合作，有助于构建更高效、更可持续的物流系统。

Abstract: The less-than-truckload (LTL) industry plays a vital role in enhancing the efficiency and sustainability of logistics systems, as LTL shipments offer greater consolidation opportunities than full-truckload shipments. Despite of this flexibility, the average cost of LTL shipments remains considerably higher due to less efficient operations and highly fragmented networks of small and medium-sized carriers. Building on our ongoing effort to develop a distributed and dynamic logistics hub network system grounded in the Physical Internet (PI) principles of modular containers and open resource sharing, this study focuses specifically on inter-hub and in-hub operations, with cooperation among multiple regional hub networks. Therefore, a shipment may traverse multiple cooperating hub networks. With respect to each hub network each shipment enters, it is defined by its expected arrival time at the entry hub and its latest arrival time at the exit hub. Based on the defined shipment information, we design a set of multi-hub operation planning protocols for distributed hub operators. In their operating networks, operators use our smartly designed protocol separately to plan in-hub shipments' assignments to destination-specific trailers and inter-hub trailers' dispatch schedules. With carefully designed interconnections between hub networks, the aggregated hub network system is well-positioned to achieve cooperative outcomes and fulfill shipment requests. We evaluate the effectiveness of the proposed protocol through a simulation-based experiment under multiple scenarios in an operator's multi-hub network. Overall, this research improves the practicality and robustness of PI-based networks and supports greater cooperation among hub networks toward more efficient and sustainable logistics systems.

</details>
